This is a current, evidence-linked queue, not a full-language denominator.
The [original gates](COVERAGE_AND_GATES.md) require interpreted lexical/semantic
scope and reviewed interactions as well as text membership. [PG14 remaining](grammar_construction/REMAINING.md),
[historical grammar-wide register](unadi_first_pada_readings/REMAINING.md) and
[ID-closure boundary](independent_written_closure/COMPLETION_AND_FRONTIER.md)
remain source records; their dated "next" counters are not current frontier
claims. In particular all 748 Uṇādi addresses now have a selected lexical
reading; do not restart at the historical 2.1 frontier. Exact immutable gate/
register/constructor pins are in [PG365 SOURCES](bhid_causative_passive_present_system_completion/SOURCES.json).
Other evidence links below identify their preserved packet; no claim of new
reverification of every linked packet is made.
